# Family Q aggregate — ELAI deep feature discovery

## Document contract

- **Family:** Q (trust-spine durability + routing-economics + distribution + research-conditioned-creativity + AIDE² instrument economics + autoresearch synergy + e2e functional suite).
- **Inputs:** `DEEP-Q-001`, `DEEP-Q-002`, and `17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md`.
- **Policy:** Every distinct atomic record is preserved with its ID and source anchor (ledger file + line). No prose compression. No silent drop of minor atoms.
- **Namespacing:** Deep atoms keep native IDs under ledger prefix `Q-001/` or `Q-002/`. Summary atoms use `SUMMARY/`. Cross-file IX/SD/AC stay namespaced by ledger (e.g. `Q-001/IX-003`, `Q-002/SD-Q10-001`).
- **NEWER-PLAN PRECEDENCE (within family):** An explicit SUPERSEDED/replacement/CORRECT/REJECTED/SHIPPED measurement marker in a source beats everything; otherwise a higher plan number is newer and wins the **target state** description. Losing descriptions are retained as superseded notes with anchors. Deep ledgers supersede the summary orientation pass where they conflict.
- **Notable status overrides (deep wins):** Q-14 E-1 **SHIPPED** 2026-07-18; Q-15 S-1 **REJECTED-by-measurement** 2026-07-17; Q-10 B-CREATIVITY-0 **VALID REFUTED** 2026-07-14 (aligned deep+summary).
- **Status language preserved:** PLANNED / SHIPPED / REJECTED-by-measurement / VALID REFUTED / DEFERRED / PENDING are not collapsed.
- **Generated:** 2026-08-12 (Q-family aggregator pass)

## Notation

- `[E]` / `E/*` explicit · `[I]` inference · `[A]` ambiguity · `[C]` conflict/correction · `[Δ]` summary delta · `[IX]` interaction · `[AC]` register · `[SUM]` summary · `[SURFACE]` user-facing · `[superseded]` losing target-state retained.
- Domain tags from deep ledgers (STATUS, CAP, SURFACE, FLOW, …) are preserved in the class field.
- Anchors: `Q-N:Lx-Ly` = Q-plan originals; `QG:Lx-Ly` = grounding map; `17-Q:Ln` = summary discovery line; ledger-line = deep/summary discovery file line.

## Summary-layer records (`17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md`)

### SUMMARY section: Q-wide intent and boundaries (15 records)

- **SUMMARY/QW-01** [SUM] [purpose] anchor=`17-Q:L34` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L34): Q is a non-product-surface letter: it hardens or measures the trust spine instead of introducing a new primary user surface.
- **SUMMARY/QW-02** [SUM] [thesis] anchor=`17-Q:L35` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L35): Q treats the deterministic harness as the product and the model as a replaceable component.
- **SUMMARY/QW-03** [SUM] [proof] anchor=`17-Q:L36` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L36): Q is intended to move PS-3, PS-4, PS-6, or K measurement evidence, depending on the unit.
- **SUMMARY/QW-04** [SUM] [authority boundary] anchor=`17-Q:L37` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L37): Research citations do not substitute for target grounding or execution.
- **SUMMARY/QW-05** [SUM] [authority boundary] anchor=`17-Q:L38` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L38): Novelty labels do not substitute for target grounding or execution.
- **SUMMARY/QW-06** [SUM] [authority boundary] anchor=`17-Q:L39` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L39): Human preference does not substitute for target grounding or execution.
- **SUMMARY/QW-07** [SUM] [authority boundary] anchor=`17-Q:L40` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L40): Generic extra deliberation does not substitute for target grounding or execution.
- **SUMMARY/QW-08** [SUM] [reuse] anchor=`17-Q:L41` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L41): Every Q unit must compose with named existing artifacts and re-implement none of them.
- **SUMMARY/QW-09** [SUM] [done-gate] anchor=`17-Q:L42` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L42): Every unit is framed by a live trigger, an off-agent artifact, and a negative case that must fail.
- **SUMMARY/QW-10** [SUM] [advisory invariant] anchor=`17-Q:L43` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L43): TD-3, “advisory-never-the-gate,” binds Q-2 and Q-3.
- **SUMMARY/QW-11** [SUM] [sequence] anchor=`17-Q:L44` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L44): Distribution hygiene is sequenced after the proof spine, specifically after PS-2/PS-4.
- **SUMMARY/QW-12** [SUM] [research sequence] anchor=`17-Q:L45` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L45): Instrumentation and spend-honest measurement must precede research-search changes.
- **SUMMARY/QW-13** [SUM] [external-claim discipline] anchor=`17-Q:L46` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L46): Every AIDE² vendor number remains `[EXTERNAL]`, never an ELAI result.
- **SUMMARY/QW-14** [SUM] [external-claim discipline] anchor=`17-Q:L47` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L47): Every Evo/NeMo mechanism remains `[EXTERNAL]` when discussed as a vendor mechanism.
- **SUMMARY/QW-15** [SUM] [no training expansion] anchor=`17-Q:L48` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L48): The autoresearch work is harness Python/Rust on the free-lane apparatus; it does not add rented-GPU or training-layer work.

### SUMMARY section: Exhaustive atomic feature inventory::Q-1 — standing goals [KEYSTONE] (22 records)

- **SUMMARY/Q1-01** [SUM] [lifecycle] anchor=`17-Q:L56` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L56): Completing a unit enrolls a persistent `STANDING GOAL`.
- **SUMMARY/Q1-02** [SUM] [predicate] anchor=`17-Q:L57` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L57): A standing goal’s predicate is the completed unit’s own frozen-checker command.
- **SUMMARY/Q1-03** [SUM] [state/data] anchor=`17-Q:L58` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L58): `crates/orchestrator` gains a goal-ledger table.
- **SUMMARY/Q1-04** [SUM] [design choice] anchor=`17-Q:L59` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L59): The goal ledger sits over O-2 `TaskSpec`s.
- **SUMMARY/Q1-05** [SUM] [immutability] anchor=`17-Q:L60` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L60): The rejected alternative is adding cadence directly to the frozen gate, because that would violate R8.
- **SUMMARY/Q1-06** [SUM] [enrollment seam] anchor=`17-Q:L61` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L61): Enrollment occurs at the FSM `COMPLETE` / auditor-`PASS` seam.
- **SUMMARY/Q1-07** [SUM] [scheduler] anchor=`17-Q:L62` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L62): Each standing goal gets an O-2 scheduled `TaskSpec` using `task_runner` intervals.
- **SUMMARY/Q1-08** [SUM] [execution] anchor=`17-Q:L63` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L63): A predicate runner re-runs the frozen checker on every scheduled cadence.
- **SUMMARY/Q1-09** [SUM] [event] anchor=`17-Q:L64` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L64): Enrollment emits `GoalEnrolled` at `DONE`.
- **SUMMARY/Q1-10** [SUM] [event] anchor=`17-Q:L65` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L65): Each scheduled successful check emits `GoalVerified`.
- **SUMMARY/Q1-11** [SUM] [failure] anchor=`17-Q:L66` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L66): A predicate exit code other than zero produces `GoalViolated`.
- **SUMMARY/Q1-12** [SUM] [event payload] anchor=`17-Q:L67` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L67): `GoalViolated` names suspected causes in `suspects`.
- **SUMMARY/Q1-13** [SUM] [safety] anchor=`17-Q:L68` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L68): The sentinel names suspects but never attempts a repair.
- **SUMMARY/Q1-14** [SUM] [safety] anchor=`17-Q:L69` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L69): A sentinel that changes files is a failing implementation.
- **SUMMARY/Q1-15** [SUM] [workflow] anchor=`17-Q:L70` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L70): A violation re-enters ELAI through the existing front door/router.
- **SUMMARY/Q1-16** [SUM] [UI] [SURFACE] anchor=`17-Q:L71` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L71): The existing intervention-card inbox renders the `GoalViolated` item.
- **SUMMARY/Q1-17** [SUM] [flakiness] anchor=`17-Q:L72` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L72): A flaky predicate is passed to F-5 quarantine.
- **SUMMARY/Q1-18** [SUM] [durability] anchor=`17-Q:L73` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L73): A flaky standing goal is never deleted merely for being flaky.
- **SUMMARY/Q1-19** [SUM] [tiering] anchor=`17-Q:L74` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L74): Ratchet-weld and compost-proposer behavior can ride on the ledger at Tier 2.
- **SUMMARY/Q1-20** [SUM] [test] anchor=`17-Q:L75` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L75): The live proof ships a unit to `COMPLETE`, reverts its wiring, advances ticks, and expects the first post-revert tick to violate.
- **SUMMARY/Q1-21** [SUM] [negative test] anchor=`17-Q:L76` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L76): Deleting a flaky goal must fail.
- **SUMMARY/Q1-22** [SUM] [dependencies] anchor=`17-Q:L77` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L77): Q-1 consumes O-2 scheduling, the SSB journal, F-5 quarantine, I-25 routing/intervention cards, the 3F rules engine, and each unit’s frozen verifier.

### SUMMARY section: Exhaustive atomic feature inventory::Q-2 — quorum wake-gate (20 records)

- **SUMMARY/Q2-01** [SUM] [trigger] anchor=`17-Q:L83` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L83): The wake-gate runs on an O-1/O-2 scheduled tick.
- **SUMMARY/Q2-02** [SUM] [voters] anchor=`17-Q:L84` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L84): It fans out to `N` cheap voters from the J free-API pool.
- **SUMMARY/Q2-03** [SUM] [diversity] anchor=`17-Q:L85` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L85): The voters must be from distinct model families.
- **SUMMARY/Q2-04** [SUM] [independence] anchor=`17-Q:L86` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L86): Voters independently decide whether the tick is actionable.
- **SUMMARY/Q2-05** [SUM] [blindness] anchor=`17-Q:L87` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L87): Each voter votes blind rather than seeing or coordinating with the other votes.
- **SUMMARY/Q2-06** [SUM] [decision] anchor=`17-Q:L88` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L88): The expensive conductor wakes only on a majority-yes result.
- **SUMMARY/Q2-07** [SUM] [placement] anchor=`17-Q:L89` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L89): Wake triage precedes the 4G `AutonomousGate` evaluator call.
- **SUMMARY/Q2-08** [SUM] [cost boundary] anchor=`17-Q:L90` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L90): The quorum gates cost/attention, never correctness.
- **SUMMARY/Q2-09** [SUM] [delay semantics] anchor=`17-Q:L91` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L91): A false-no can delay work only until a later tick.
- **SUMMARY/Q2-10** [SUM] [in-flight safety] anchor=`17-Q:L92` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L92): A voter must not suppress an in-flight result.
- **SUMMARY/Q2-11** [SUM] [quiet behavior] anchor=`17-Q:L93` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L93): A non-actionable tick has approximately voter-only cost and must not wake the expensive conductor.
- **SUMMARY/Q2-12** [SUM] [actionable behavior] anchor=`17-Q:L94` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L94): An actionable tick wakes the conductor exactly once.
- **SUMMARY/Q2-13** [SUM] [event] anchor=`17-Q:L95` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L95): Every voter emits `WakeQuorumVoted`; there are `N` distinct-family rows per tick.
- **SUMMARY/Q2-14** [SUM] [event] anchor=`17-Q:L96` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L96): Every tick emits one `WakeGated` row.
- **SUMMARY/Q2-15** [SUM] [event data] anchor=`17-Q:L97` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L97): `WakeGated.woke` is false on the quiet case and true on the actionable case.
- **SUMMARY/Q2-16** [SUM] [metric] anchor=`17-Q:L98` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L98): Wake precision must be recomputable from the journal/SSB evidence.
- **SUMMARY/Q2-17** [SUM] [self-removal] anchor=`17-Q:L99` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L99): At Tier 2, the filter self-uninstalls if measured wake precision proves it redundant.
- **SUMMARY/Q2-18** [SUM] [UI/ops] [SURFACE] anchor=`17-Q:L100` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L100): There is no new frontend; evidence is exposed through SSB rows and the existing dispatch-ledger view.
- **SUMMARY/Q2-19** [SUM] [negative test] anchor=`17-Q:L101` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L101): A quiet tick that still wakes must fail.
- **SUMMARY/Q2-20** [SUM] [dependencies] anchor=`17-Q:L102` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L102): Q-2 consumes O-1 daemon/O-2 ticks, 4G gate evaluation, J free-API voters, I-25 routing, D-13’s cross-family principle, and the SSB measured-number idiom.

### SUMMARY constraints and invariants

- **SUMMARY/CONST-01** [SUM/CONST] applies=`Q-1, Q-6, Q-9` anchor=`17-Q:L661` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L661): **Frozen checker remains independent** → Do not mutate the checker/corpus from the judged run; output is an off-agent event or exit code.
- **SUMMARY/CONST-02** [SUM/CONST] applies=`Q-2, Q-3, Q-14` anchor=`17-Q:L662` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L662): **Advisory never gates correctness** → Quorum controls wake cost, advice cannot be a pass verdict, conjecture triage stays out of the gate manifest.
- **SUMMARY/CONST-03** [SUM/CONST] applies=`Q-1, Q-6, Q-11, Q-13, Q-14` anchor=`17-Q:L663` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L663): **Missing/dead evidence fails closed** → Nonzero standing predicate violates; corrupt/missing gate evidence blocks; failed canaries suppress; unresolved briefing lines drop.
- **SUMMARY/CONST-04** [SUM/CONST] applies=`Q-3, Q-11, Q-12, Q-14, Q-15` anchor=`17-Q:L664` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L664): **Measured cost is consumed, not re-derived** → Use spend/usage rows for economics, equal-budget checks, and cost tie-breaks.
- **SUMMARY/CONST-05** [SUM/CONST] applies=`Q-11, Q-12, Q-15` anchor=`17-Q:L665` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L665): **Spend cannot manufacture a win** → A spend-leaking result is flagged and cannot promote.
- **SUMMARY/CONST-06** [SUM/CONST] applies=`Q-7, Q-12, Q-14, Q-15` anchor=`17-Q:L666` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L666): **Within-noise is not a win** → No style/bandit promotion; quantified kills are reinstatable; underpowered nulls are not refutations.
- **SUMMARY/CONST-07** [SUM/CONST] applies=`Q-10` anchor=`17-Q:L667` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L667): **Source evidence has source-only authority** → Relation/target claims require independent target anchors and execution.
- **SUMMARY/CONST-08** [SUM/CONST] applies=`Q-4` anchor=`17-Q:L668` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L668): **Model-conditioned work cannot cross a swap** → Drop prior turns/diffs/commitments; retain only `BeliefState`; journal discarded work.
- **SUMMARY/CONST-09** [SUM/CONST] applies=`Q-5` anchor=`17-Q:L669` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L669): **Lane-specific replay claims** → Same-class local tokens are hashed; cloud is action-stream only; cloud rows have no token hash.
- **SUMMARY/CONST-10** [SUM/CONST] applies=`Q-8` anchor=`17-Q:L670` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L670): **Pending numbers cannot block** → Measure/report until a real baseline sets a ceiling; only a set ceiling blocks.
- **SUMMARY/CONST-11** [SUM/CONST] applies=`Q-6, Q-9, Q-13, Q-15` anchor=`17-Q:L671` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L671): **One implementation/source** → One corruption generator, one command registry, reuse citation types/resolver, one variance helper.
- **SUMMARY/CONST-12** [SUM/CONST] applies=`Q-7, Q-10, Q-12, Q-15` anchor=`17-Q:L672` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L672): **Experiments do not silently become defaults** → Promotion requires the specified gates; refuted/underpowered work remains non-runtime.
- **SUMMARY/CONST-13** [SUM/CONST] applies=`Q-3, Q-11, Q-12, Q-15` anchor=`17-Q:L673` (ledger-line: 17-Q-SUMMARY_INDEX.discovery.md:L673): **External numbers remain external** → Never relabel vendor figures or mechanisms as ELAI results.
